# Benchmark programme — tracked state memo

Tracked copy of the unit table in `cruxvault/results/t81/PROGRAMME_STATE.md` (gitignored, per-worktree).
The gitignored file is the live one; this memo is refreshed when a phase closes. Brief:
`plan/HANDOFF_FINISH_BENCHMARK.md`. Branch: `implementation/t81-finish-benchmark` (the PI merges it to main).

**Interim render 3, 2026-09-03 07:41 PDT.** The programme is still running: this is a snapshot of the
rows, not a close-out. The previous render was 180 rows at 2026-09-02 21:19 PDT.

## Decisions recorded 2026-09-01

| # | decision |
|---|---|
| D1 | `marginal`, `knn1`, `knn5` run once per regime; `avg`, `avg-arcsinh` once. 18 method-regime units. §12.2/§12.3 to be corrected; identity assertion for `avg`/`avg-arcsinh` to be added. |
| D2 | Build the training-residual σ pass; repoint all four rivals' `fit_sigma.py` and `avg-arcsinh` at it. |
| D3 | Truth toggle does not apply to `V_`. Exclusion recorded on the board. |
| D4 | `eic.19` band sized from a fresh measured epoch rate, `--early-stop-epochs 3`, 25-epoch ceiling. |
| D5 | The 40 `counts.h5.pre_t78p3` backups stay until every retrain is scored. |
| ops | Fir code: fresh clone `/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/CANDII_main` on this branch. Venv: `/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/EpiDenoise/candi_venv`. t90: rsync via a SLURM CPU job, checksum-verified, scratch copy left to purge. |

K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. THIS IS THE RELAUNCH of the four seed-0 units chunk W3-candi-cpu submitted at 04:30 on 2026-09-03 and lost, 92 tasks out of 92, to two launcher defects: Defect 1, the positional sed -n 1p/2p/3p capture of $PLAN off a stdout the library also writes to; Defect 2, the non-atomic derived-regime write that 12 concurrent shards raced on. K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

THIS IS THE RELAUNCH of the four seed-0 units chunk W3-candi-cpu submitted at 04:30 on 2026-09-03 and lost, 92 tasks out of 92, to two launcher defects: Defect 1, the positional sed -n 1p/2p/3p capture of $PLAN off a stdout the library also writes to; Defect 2, the non-atomic derived-regime write that 12 concurrent shards raced on. K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. REGIME=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_pred_B/CANDI/eic_pilot/regime.eic_pilot.B_.json, PRED=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_pred_B/CANDI/eic_pilot/B_, TRUTH_ROOT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_truth_challenge/B_, OUT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_scores/CANDI/eic_pilot/challenge.B_.json; --gres=none --time=03:00:00 --dependency=afterok:57953589, the MERGE. The challenge truth root covers BOTH regimes: eic_19 and eic_pilot declare the IDENTICAL 12 B_ pairs, so this pass is viable and was not blocked. Band cpubase_bycore_b1. Target json absent at submit. STATE queued: PENDING (Dependency) all sitting. Kit KIT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/CANDII_main at HEAD 0518d9d (K16: CANDI predict plan is read by marker with library stdout redirected; declare_eval_pairs split writes atomically); grep -c PLAN:chroms slurm/t81_predict_candi.sh = 3, so K16 is on the kit and no pull was needed. THIS IS THE RELAUNCH of the four seed-0 units chunk W3-candi-cpu submitted at 04:30 on 2026-09-03 and lost, 92 tasks out of 92, to two launcher defects: Defect 1, the positional sed -n 1p/2p/3p capture of $PLAN off a stdout the library also writes to; Defect 2, the non-atomic derived-regime write that 12 concurrent shards raced on. K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

WHY THE STORE PASS ALSO CARRIES afterany ON ITS CHALLENGE SIBLING, unchanged from 02:05: both B_ score jobs of one regime derive their panel regime to the SAME path - _env.sh:122 sets PANEL_REGIME=$WS/regime.$REGIME_NAME.$PANEL.json with no override hook - and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py:316 writes it with Path.write_text, which truncates before it writes, so SCORES_BASELINES_B.md 2.2(ii)'s precedent is to serialize rather than race. The comma is an AND and both halves are load-bearing: afterany:57955929 alone would be satisfied by a challenge job Slurm cancelled for DependencyNeverSatisfied, and the store pass would then run against a root its array never finished writing; afterok:57955927 forbids that. The script's --time=24:00:00 is kept and no path was overridden by hand: PRED defaults to _env.sh's PRED_B and OUT to $SCORES_DIR/<truth>.B_.json. Every variable exported in the shell with a bare --export=ALL, so no assignment sits inside an --export list. HELD-OUT SCOPE ONLY: BENCHMARK_DESIGN.md 4 blanks Avocado's genome-wide cell and score.sh passes --chroms chr20,chr21,chr22 explicitly, so genome_wide_computed must be false. WHEN IT LANDS read off the json: provenance.truth.source, provenance.sigma_table.fitted_on, len(tracks) against provenance.declared_tracks (the running predict tasks confirm 51), missing_tracks, allow_missing, provenance.point_only_tracks (the sibling methods show a 48+3 split, the 3 being ATAC-seq targets the sigma table has no row for - READ it, do not assume), panels.pval.B n_experiments, panels.count.B present and empty, panels.peak ABSENT, genome_wide_computed false. NOT a quotable number when it lands - no oracle_scaled/scale_error split and the noise floor is unmeasured (t86); target-clustered floor ~0.09 on macro CRPS, and a seed change alone moves pooled CRPS 0.1195 (AGENTS.md 7.2). THE LINE: same REGIME and PRED as the challenge pass, TRUTH_ROOT unset, export SCOPE=genomewide OUT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_scores/CANDI/eic_19/store.B_.json; sbatch --parsable --export=ALL --gres=none --time=60:00:00 --dependency=afterok:57953582,afterany:57953583 slurm/t81_score_external.sh -> 57953584. THE TWO-CLAUSE DEPENDENCY LANDED INTACT, verified on the queue: Dependency=afterok:57953582(unfulfilled),afterany:57953583(unfulfilled). afterok on the MERGE is the correctness clause; afterany on the challenge pass 57953583 only serialises the two so they do not read one root at once. Band 60:00:00 = cpubase_bycore_b4, --mem=32G --cpus-per-task=4. No SIGMA. Target json absent at submit. STATE queued: PENDING (Dependency) all sitting. Kit KIT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/CANDII_main at HEAD 0518d9d (K16: CANDI predict plan is read by marker with library stdout redirected; declare_eval_pairs split writes atomically); grep -c PLAN:chroms slurm/t81_predict_candi.sh = 3, so K16 is on the kit and no pull was needed. THIS IS THE RELAUNCH of the four seed-0 units chunk W3-candi-cpu submitted at 04:30 on 2026-09-03 and lost, 92 tasks out of 92, to two launcher defects: Defect 1, the positional sed -n 1p/2p/3p capture of $PLAN off a stdout the library also writes to; Defect 2, the non-atomic derived-regime write that 12 concurrent shards raced on. K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

THIS IS THE RELAUNCH of the four seed-0 units chunk W3-candi-cpu submitted at 04:30 on 2026-09-03 and lost, 92 tasks out of 92, to two launcher defects: Defect 1, the positional sed -n 1p/2p/3p capture of $PLAN off a stdout the library also writes to; Defect 2, the non-atomic derived-regime write that 12 concurrent shards raced on. K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

THE LINE: export REGIME=/scratch/mforooz/t81_pred/CANDI/eic_19/regime.eic_19.V_.json; export PRED=/scratch/mforooz/t81_pred/CANDI/eic_19/V_cpu; export OUT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_scores/CANDI/eic_19/store.V_.json; export SCOPE=genomewide HELD_OUT_CHROMS=chr20,chr21,chr22; sbatch --parsable --export=ALL --gres=none --time=60:00:00 --dependency=afterok:57953579 slurm/t81_score_external.sh -> 57953580. Chained on the MERGE 57953579, never on the array 57953578. Band 60:00:00 = cpubase_bycore_b4, --mem=32G --cpus-per-task=4 (the script's defaults). SCOPE=genomewide makes ONE pass carry TWO aggregations: macro and panels stay held-out over chr20-22, and genome_wide.macro / genome_wide.panels roll up all 23 chromosomes the root carries; the launcher adds --held-out-chroms for that. No SIGMA: CANDI emits its own, so a sigma table would be wrong here. The target json did not exist at submit time and neither did its directory: /project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_scores/ carries no CANDI/ at all. STATE queued: PENDING (Dependency) all sitting, no log file, nothing at the out path. Kit KIT=/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/CANDII_main at HEAD 0518d9d (K16: CANDI predict plan is read by marker with library stdout redirected; declare_eval_pairs split writes atomically); grep -c PLAN:chroms slurm/t81_predict_candi.sh = 3, so K16 is on the kit and no pull was needed. THIS IS THE RELAUNCH of the four seed-0 units chunk W3-candi-cpu submitted at 04:30 on 2026-09-03 and lost, 92 tasks out of 92, to two launcher defects: Defect 1, the positional sed -n 1p/2p/3p capture of $PLAN off a stdout the library also writes to; Defect 2, the non-atomic derived-regime write that 12 concurrent shards raced on. K16 fixes both - the plan heredoc now runs under contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr) and emits PLAN:chroms= / PLAN:n_tracks= / PLAN:want_time= markers that bash greps for by NAME, so position is no longer a contract, and tools/declare_eval_pairs.py split writes tmp + os.replace. CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

CPU-SHARDED MODE: DEVICE=cpu SHARD_CHROMS=1, --array=0-22%12 --gres=none --cpus-per-task=8 --mem=16G --time=1-00:00:00 (cpubase_bycore_b3), then a SHARD_MERGE=1 SHARD_ARRAY=<array id> job afterok on the array (--gres=none --cpus-per-task=1 --mem=8G --time=00:20:00) that verifies every declared track x planned chromosome holds an npz and writes the ONE manifest.json, adding device= and sharded: true with the array id to notes. Scorers chain afterok on the MERGE, never on the array, because a one-chromosome manifest can sit in the root mid-array and the scorer reads manifest.chroms. Every variable is exported in the shell and every submit passes bare --export=ALL (a comma-valued variable inside --export=ALL,VAR=a,b,c arrives truncated at the first comma). ALL 20 SUBMISSIONS WENT IN FROM ONE ssh SHELL, so every dependency was taken against a live job id captured in that shell; scontrol confirmed all 20 dependencies landed intact, including the two two-clause ones. sbatch --test-only was run once per distinct shape (array / merge / genome-wide score / held-out challenge score) and all four validated; --gres=none is accepted and overrides the script's #SBATCH --gres. The four seed-0 arrays are staggered --begin now / +10 / +20 / +30 minutes, because more than 12 simultaneous torch imports off the shared /project venv fail with partial-module ImportErrors; %12 caps one array and the stagger keeps the four first bursts from coinciding. The two seed-1 arrays are chained --dependency=afterany on the seed-0 eic_19 array of the SAME panel, so at most four arrays ever run at once. THE BOARD MUST SAY WHICH ROOT AND WHICH DEVICE A CANDI ROW CAME FROM: three prediction roots per regime now carry method: CANDI in their manifest - the canonical GPU V_, the CPU V_cpu, and CANDI_s1 - because the launcher has no METHOD variable and --method CANDI is a literal, so entrant identity lives in the PATHS. No scancel was run by this chunk; no root, checkpoint or json was deleted or overwritten; no tracked file was edited; no git commit was made.  | 2026-09-03 |

## Programme-wide caveats

Facts about how these runs were produced, not judgements about what they show. Each one names
where it is on the record.

- **No number on this page is a result.** Every board view still compiles `unranked`, because
  the CANDII noise floor is unmeasured (t86). The target-clustered noise floor on macro CRPS is
  ~0.09 and a seed change alone moves pooled CRPS by 0.1195 (`AGENTS.md` §7.2), both larger than
  several recorded between-arm gaps, so no gap is readable. `AGENTS.md` §7.2 also forbids a raw
  CRPS without its `oracle_scaled` / `scale_error` split. The `number` column is an address into
  a run, not a score to quote.
- **The seed-1 CANDI run is a floor measurement, never a board row.** `train.CANDI.eic_19.s1`
  (57865895_0) COMPLETED in 13:45:29, best epoch 5, early stop at epoch 11, and is staged at
  `/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_checkpoints/t81_eic_19_s1.{best.ckpt,json}`. It exists to size
  the seed-to-seed spread (`cruxvault/results/t86/RUN.md`). One CANDI row per regime.
- **CANDI predictions run on CPU, sharded by chromosome, because the GPU queue starved.** The
  `def-maxwl_gpu` pool gave no start estimate from 19:25 PDT on 2026-09-02; no CANDI predict had
  started 9 h later, and one eDICE `B_` pass had waited about 14 h. K15 added a CPU-sharded mode
  to `slurm/t81_predict_candi.sh`: `DEVICE=cpu` (it sets the OMP/MKL thread count from the
  allocated cores), `SHARD_CHROMS=1` turns the pass into `--array=0-22`, one chromosome per task,
  writing `.shard_manifest.<chrom>.json`, and a separate `SHARD_MERGE=1` job verifies every
  track × chromosome before it writes the one root `manifest.json`. The passes now running are
  those arrays: seed 0 eic_19 `V_` 57953578 and `B_` 57953581, eic_pilot `V_` 57953585 and `B_`
  57953588; seed 1 eic_19 `V_` 57953592 and `B_` 57953595, each chained behind the seed-0 array
  of the same panel. Measured shard wall time is 1:15–1:35 h per chromosome at 8 cores, so an
  array is about 3 h.
- **The CANDI `V_` roots are `V_cpu` siblings, not the canonical `V_`.** The GPU replacement
  predicts 57944437 (eic_19) and 57944438 (eic_pilot) were still queued on the canonical roots
  and the PI had not ruled on cancelling them, so the CPU passes write
  `/scratch/mforooz/t81_pred/CANDI/<regime>/V_cpu` rather than a root another job might claim.
  The `B_` CPU passes write the canonical `B_` root under
  `/project/def-maxwl/mforooz/t81_pred_B/CANDI/<regime>/`: no `B_` pass was ever submitted on
  GPU, and the once-guard markers `.b_once.57953581` and `.b_once.57953588` are claimed. Seed 1
  writes its own `CANDI_s1` roots and does not touch either.
- **Three launcher defects were found on the night of 2026-09-02/03. All three are fixed,
  tested and merged — one in chunk K14, two in chunk K16, with the CPU-shard chunk K15 between
  them.** The CANDI predict launcher had **never run end-to-end** before tonight, so every CANDI
  predict submitted before the last fix was dead at its first step and wrote nothing — no root,
  no marker, no npz, no json, and no `B_` once-budget spent.
  - **K14** — `slurm/t81_predict_candi.sh` asked for `CANDI_STORE/eic/genome/chrom_sizes.json`,
    which does not exist: the genome layer is `CANDI_STORE/genome`. This is the same path bug
    K13 had already fixed in the scorer. With the chromosome list empty the PLAN step exited 1
    at start. The fix takes the list from the corpus genome directory intersected with what the
    store actually holds (`CorpusStore.n_bins()`): the corpus manifest lists 23 chromosomes, the
    shared genome layer 24, and the store holds no chrY. Merged as `b506bd3`.
  - **K15** — the CPU-sharded mode above. Not itself a defect fix; it made the K14-class failure
    exit early and legibly instead of running garbage.
  - **K16, defect one** — `harness.py:701` prints its `[bench] N declared eval pair(s)` banner to
    **stdout**, and the launcher captured the PLAN heredoc's stdout positionally, so the
    chromosome list became that banner. Present since chunk C (`1633756`). The fix puts the PLAN
    body under `redirect_stdout(stderr)` and emits its values as `PLAN:<name>=` lines the
    launcher reads by marker.
  - **K16, defect two** — `tools/declare_eval_pairs.py:316-317` wrote the derived regime file
    non-atomically and then re-read it, and 12 shards shared one `$WORKSPACE/regime.<r>.<P>.json`.
    The fix writes through `mkstemp` + `os.replace`. Merged as `0518d9d`.
  - What the defects cost: 6 GPU predicts (57910740, 57914202, 57943014, 57943025, 57944437,
    57944438, plus the chained 57942777/57942786/57943015/57943029) and 92 CPU shard tasks
    (86 exit 6, 6 exit 1). SLURM dropped 10 dependents itself as `DependencyNeverSatisfied`.
- **Avocado ran on two devices, and that is recorded on both boards.** `V_` predictions ran on
  GPU under bf16 autocast; `B_` predictions ran on CPU in fp32, because `autocast` self-disables
  on CPU (`competitors/avocado/predict.py:133`, and its device fall-back at `:71`). The device
  note is `cruxvault/results/t81/W3_AVOCADO.md` §26. The CPU `B_` pass cost **13–19 min per
  chromosome** per regime at 8 cores — it needs chr20-22 only — against a GPU pair
  (57942789 eic_19, 57942792 eic_pilot) that had been pending for hours; those two will now hit
  the launcher's once-guard and exit 4 before any write, and their four chained scores drop.
  Avocado is complete: all five addressable cells on both boards.
- **One effective code state for the jobs queued across the fixes.** Jobs submitted while the
  Fir kit was at `bd71b25` banner a newer sha when they start. The commits in between are
  leaderboard rows plus the K14 launcher fix, and neither changes any prediction or scoring code
  path those jobs run, so the queue is one effective code state, not two.
- **Two baselines have no count arm.** `avg-arcsinh` and Avocado predict `-log10 p` only, so
  `macro.count` is empty and their fills needed `--allow-missing`; the ATAC-seq targets are
  point-only for the same class of reason. Recorded as caveats on both boards by the stamp-6,
  stamp-8, stamp-9 and stamp-10 passes.
- **The D1 collapse is a fact about the eic.pilot board, not a bug.** Under decision D1 the
  `avg` and `avg-arcsinh` rows on `eic.pilot` are the `eic_19` generation root scored at pilot
  addresses; only `avg-arcsinh` names a σ table (`sigma.eic_19.json`) — `avg` fits none. A
  factual caveat to that effect was added on `eic.pilot` by the stamp-10 pass.
- **The Avocado score launcher has a race, and it was worked around, not fixed.** Its
  `score.sh` writes its derived `PANEL_REGIME` file non-atomically, so two same-regime score
  jobs can collide; the store pass was serialised behind the challenge pass with `afterany`
  instead. Left on record for a docs pass.
